_source line 377 · 2026-05-13T22:06:29.469Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   Train a ModernBERT-base synthetic text detector on remote server westd-47453 (root account), then perform a gate check on the results. Specifically:
   - Prepare data directories (separate from other data to avoid loading wrong files)
   - Copy human detector split (67,960 lines) and synthetic shard_0 (85,248 lines) to dedicated directories
   - Train using single GPU (cuda:0) with specific hyperparameters
   - After training, verify: AUC >= 0.95 and ECE <= 0.05
   - Report: training success, gate check results (AUC, ECE, temperature), model path, training duration, and full calibration_results.json if gate fails

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - ModernBERT-base (answerdotai/ModernBERT-base) for sequence classification
   - TORCHDYNAMO_DISABLE=1 required for torch 2.10.0 compatibility
   - HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1 to avoid xet_get 401 errors on AutoDL
   - AutoDL network proxy via `/etc/network_turbo` (http_proxy=http://10.37.1.23:12798)
   - SSL certificate settings for AutoDL proxy
   - HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache to avoid filling root partition
   - Conda environment at /root/miniconda3 (not venv)
   - deepspeed/torch._inductor compatibility issue requiring deepspeed removal
   - HuggingFace Trainer API with eval_steps=500
   - Temperature calibration for detector model

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/provenance_weight_training/run_detector_train.sh` (CREATED)
     - Shell script created to properly embed all environment variables for nohup execution
     - Solves the problem of proxy env vars not being inherited by nohup subprocesses
     ```bash
     #!/bin/bash
     set -e
     cd /root/provenance_weight_training
     export no_proxy=localhost,127.0.0.1,modelscope.com,aliyuncs.com,tencentyun.com,wisemodel.cn
     export http_proxy=http://10.37.1.23:12798
     export https_proxy=http://10.37.1.23:12798
     export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
     export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
     source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh
     conda activate base
     export TORCHDYNAMO_DISABLE=1
     export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1
     export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0
     export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache
     echo "=== Starting detector training ==="
     echo "Time: $(date)"
     echo "Python: $(python --version)"
     python detector/train_detector.py \
         --human_data_dir data/detector_input/human \
         --synthetic_data_dir data/detector_input/synthetic \
         --model_name answerdotai/ModernBERT-base \
         --output_dir output/detector \
         --max_length 512 \
         --batch_size 32 \
         --learning_rate 2e-5 \
         --num_epochs 3 \
         --warmup_ratio 0.1 \
         --eval_steps 500 \
         --seed 42
     echo "=== Training finished ==="
     echo "Time: $(date)"
     ```
   - `/root/provenance_weight_training/data/detector_input/human/detector.jsonl` (CREATED via cp)
     - 67,960 lines, copy of data/human/detector.jsonl
   - `/root/provenance_weight_training/data/detector_input/synthetic/synthetic.jsonl` (CREATED via cp)
     - 85,248 lines, copy of data/synthetic/shard_0/depth_1_nucleus_p0.95_t0.8.jsonl
   - `/root/provenance_weight_training/detector/train_detector.py` (existing, not modified)
     - The training script
   - `/tmp/detector_train.log` (training output log)
     - Contains all training progress

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **ssh_read_only shell metacharacter restriction**: Commands with `&&`, `>`, `2>&1` are blocked. Fix: Split into individual commands or use ssh_execute for complex commands.
   - **HuggingFace Network Unreachable (Attempt 1)**: First nohup training failed because proxy env vars from `source /etc/network_turbo` were not inherited. Fix: Created a standalone shell script with all env vars embedded.
   - **SSH timeout on nohup launch (Attempt 2)**: ssh_execute timed out at 30 seconds while starting the nohup process, but the process did start (PID was echoed). This was informational, not a real failure.
   - **deepspeed/torch._inductor AssertionError "duplicate template name" (Attempt 3)**: deepspeed 0.18.9 had compatibility issues with torch 2.10.0's inductor module. The import chain: Trainer → accelerate.unwrap_model → deepspeed.DeepSpeedEngine → deepspeed.compile.inductor → torch._inductor.lowering → flex_attention → TritonTemplate duplicate. Fix: `pip uninstall deepspeed -y` since single GPU training doesn't need deepspeed.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Solved AutoDL network proxy setup by embedding proxy vars directly in script
   - Solved deepspeed compatibility by removing it
   - Solved nohup env variable inheritance by using a standalone script
   - Training is currently IN PROGRESS and has not completed yet

6. All user messages:
   - Initial task message: Detailed instructions to train ModernBERT-base detector including data preparation commands, training command with all hyperparameters, post-training verification script, and expected deliverables (training success, gate check AUC/ECE/temperature, model path, duration, calibration JSON if gate fails)

7. Pending Tasks:

   - Verify model files at output/detector/final_model/

At last check the training was at step ~1957/10194 (19.2%), elapsed ~4:27, running at ~9.09 it/s. The process has been running for ~6 minutes total.

9. Optional Next Step:
